tmp_path_factory = TempPathFactory(_given_basetemp=None, _trace=<pluggy._tracing.TagTracerSub object at 0x7fd6cb9466d0>, _basetemp=PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-55'), _retention_count=3, _retention_policy='all')

cad/generate.py:199: in build_sheet
    sd = BUILDERS[content](sheet, ctx)
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"

E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

cad/generate.py:199: in build_sheet
    sd = BUILDERS[content](sheet, ctx)
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^

        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)

            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))

            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

cad/generate.py:199: in build_sheet
    sd = BUILDERS[content](sheet, ctx)
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

tmp_path_factory = TempPathFactory(_given_basetemp=None, _trace=<pluggy._tracing.TagTracerSub object at 0x7fd6cb9466d0>, _basetemp=PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-55'), _retention_count=3, _retention_policy='all')
world_master = {'$schema': './world.schema.json', 'activitySlots': [{'activity': 'desk', 'capacity': 1, 'facing': 90, 'fixture': 'FX-...loud', 'b_exclude_categories': ['service', 'shaft', 'restricted'], 'b_noise': 'quiet', 'id': 'ADJ-06', ...}, ...], ...}

tests/py/test_cad_m3.py:40: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 
cad/generate.py:229: in build_set
    r = build_sheet(sh, world, out_dxf, out_pdf, out_prev, generated_utc, world_sha, previews=previews, ctx=ctx)

cad/generate.py:199: in build_sheet
    sd = BUILDERS[content](sheet, ctx)
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

cad/generate.py:199: in build_sheet
    sd = BUILDERS[content](sheet, ctx)
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)

cad/generate.py:199: in build_sheet
    sd = BUILDERS[content](sheet, ctx)
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"

                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

sheet = {'content': 'furniture', 'discipline': 'A', 'floor': 'L1', 'id': 'A-201', ...}
ctx = {'page_counts': {'A-101': 1, 'A-102': 1, 'A-103': 2}, 'portmap': {'assumptions': ['AS-ICT-02', 'AS-ICT-03'], 'bom': {'...eblock': {'north': 'Y+ = utara', 'units': 'mm (DXF), m (label)', 'watermark': 'KONSEP - BUKAN UNTUK KONSTRUKSI'}}, ...}

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []

            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"

    def build_furniture(sheet, ctx):
        world = ctx["world"]
        floor = sheet["floor"]
        den = int(sheet["scale"].split(":")[1])
        plan = build_plan(world, floor, den, with_tags=False, paper=sheet["size"])
        sd = SheetDoc(sheet, world, make_meta(sheet, world, ctx, floor))
        page = sd.new_page()
        lay = plan_paper_layout(sheet, plan, den)
        (fx0, fy0, fx1, fy1), tb = frame_and_title(sd, page, 1, 1, ctx["utc"], ctx["sha"],
                                                   tb_origin=(lay["panel"][0], lay["frame"][1]), tb_w=150)
        page.line((lay["panel"][0], fy0), (lay["panel"][0], fy1), "A-ANNO-TTLB", stroke=GREEN, width=0.8)
        z = lay["zone"]
        v = page.view("plan", den, lay["origin"], (z[0] + 1, z[1] + 1, z[2] - 1, z[3] - 1))
        plan_base(v, plan, mode="full", fixtures="full", window_tags=True)
        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):

            mpm = v.m_per_mm()

            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05

                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))

        wall_rects = [w["rect"] for w in plan["walls"]]
        fx_boxes = []
        for fx in plan["fixtures"]:
            pts = fixture_corners(fx)
            fx_boxes.append((min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts), max(p[0] for p in pts),
                             max(p[1] for p in pts)))
        room_id_labels(v, plan, wall_rects, size=6.2, obstacles=fx_boxes)
        # Fixture keys: the numeric suffix of the ID, placed inside the footprint when it fits.
        placer = LabelPlacer(v, obstacles=[r for r in wall_rects] + outside_strips(plan))
        all_fx = plan["fixtures"] + [f for f in world["fixtures"] if f["floor"] == floor
                                     and world["catalog"][f["type"]].get("family") == "shell"]
        key_size = 4.4
        worst = 0.0
        for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"]):
            key = fx["id"].split("-")[-1]
            mpm = v.m_per_mm()
            w = text_w_mm(key, key_size, "B") * mpm + 0.08
            h = key_size / PT_PER_MM * mpm * 1.05
            cx, cy = fx["pos"]
            center = (cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2)
            box, hit = placer.place((cx, cy), key, key_size, "B", gap=0.12, extra_cands=[center])
            worst = max(worst, hit)
            v.text((box[0] + w / 2 - 0.04, box[1] + h * 0.22), key, "A-FURN-IDEN", size=key_size, font="B",
                   color="#4a423b", align="c", knock=True, xd=("fixture", fx["id"]))
        sd.summary["key_overlap_m2_max"] = round(worst, 4)
        sd.summary["fixtures"] = len(all_fx)
        # Title strip and scale.
        view_title(page, fx0 + 6, fy0 + 17, sheet["title"].upper(), f"Skala {sheet['scale']} @ {sheet['size']}")
        page.text((fx0 + 6, fy0 + 9), "Angka pada furniture = 3 digit akhir ID fixture (FX-" + floor + "-nnn); "
                  "daftar lengkap di panel kanan. Segi enam Wnn = jendela W-" + floor + "-0nn (schedule A-103).",
                  "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7, color=INK)
        scale_bar(page, fx0 + 175, fy0 + 12, den, (0, 1, 2, 5, 10))
        # Panel.
        px0, px1 = lay["panel"][0] + 5, lay["panel"][2] - 5
        pw = px1 - px0
        y = fy1 - 4
        north_arrow(page, px0 + 8, y - 11, r=6)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 8), "Utara = sumbu Y+ dunia", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=8.5, font="B", color=INK)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 12.5), f"Lantai {floor}: {floor_of(world, floor)['name']}", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        page.text((px0 + 20, y - 16.5), "Ukuran furniture = target catalog (AS-DIM-04).", "A-ANNO-NOTE", size=7)
        y -= 24
        # Type legend with counts.
        counts = {}
        for fx in all_fx:
            counts[fx["type"]] = counts.get(fx["type"], 0) + 1
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"JENIS FIXTURE ({len(counts)} jenis, {len(all_fx)} unit)", pw)
        trows = []
        for t in sorted(counts, key=lambda k: (-counts[k], k)):
            cat = world["catalog"][t]
            trows.append([cat["label"], f"{cat['size'][0]:g} x {cat['size'][1]:g}".replace(".", ","), str(counts[t])])
        half = (len(trows) + 1) // 2
        tcols = [Col("Jenis (catalog)", 41), Col("Ukuran m", 18), Col("n", 11, "r", "M")]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, tcols, trows[:half], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, tcols, trows[half:], size=5.9, lead=1.12)
        y = min(y1, y2) - 4
        y = heading(page, px0, y, f"DAFTAR FIXTURE {floor} (ID lengkap)", pw)
        rooms = {r["id"]: r["id"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in world["rooms"]}
        frows = [[fx["id"], world["catalog"][fx["type"]]["label"], rooms.get(fx["room"], fx["room"])]
                 for fx in sorted(all_fx, key=lambda f: f["id"])]
        half = (len(frows) + 1) // 2
        fcols = [Col("ID", 17, "l", "B"), Col("Jenis", 36), Col("Ruang", 17)]
        _, y1 = draw_table(page, px0, y, fcols, frows[:half], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        _, y2 = draw_table(page, px0 + 72, y, fcols, frows[half:], size=5.5, lead=1.1, zebra=True,
                           row_xd=lambda row: ("fixture", row[0]))
        y = min(y1, y2) - 3
>       assert y > tb[3] + 2, f"{sheet['id']}: panel overflows title block ({y:.1f} <= {tb[3]:.1f})"
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AssertionError: A-201: panel overflows title block (-55.4 <= 78.7)
